enacted with humility. Religion is active here as an ordering relation rather than merely a named doctrine.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Devotional Dependence and Rule","parent_semantic_invariant":"A dependent community turns toward a sovereign divine addressee through worship, service, obedience, and petition.","parent_surprising_reach":"The devotional relation extends lexically from willing worship into ownership and coercive service, while lordship also carries the countervailing function of nurture.","key":"C","name":"Petition for Aid and Direction","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Dependents seek assistance and gentle direction toward an upright path, with compassionate benefaction as the answering function.","active_motifs":"requested aid and cooperation `ع و ن:B001/m01`; gentle guidance `ه د ي:B001/m01`; the traversable path `ص ر ط:B001/m01`; uprightness and rectitude `ق و م:B008/m01`; mercy enacted as benefaction `ر ح م:B001/m02`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا`, `ٱلصِّرَٰطَ`, and `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ه د ي`, `ص ر ط`, `ق و م`); 1:7 `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`)","synthesis":"Aid supplies the enabling force, guidance supplies the directing action, the path supplies its object, and straightness supplies the desired state. Mercy turns the request-response structure into beneficent rather than merely administrative rule.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Benefaction, Welfare, and Praise","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good is conferred through compassion and maintenance, stabilized as welfare, and answered by praise or recognition.","parent_surprising_reach":"Tenderness expands into practical provisioning, repair, guardianship, and structural support, while praise can function either as gratitude or as a claim imposed by a patron.","key":"A","name":"Compassionate Provision and Nurture","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A caregiver feels tenderness, nurtures a dependent over time, guards its affairs, and supplies or repairs what sustains a good condition.","active_motifs":"tenderness and compassion `ر ح م:B001/m01`; beneficent mercy `ر ح م:B001/m02`; conferred blessing `ن ع م:B001/m02`; good condition `ن ع م:B001/m01`; progressive nurture `ر ب ب:B002/m02`; guardianship and care `ق و م:B004/m01`; provision for dependents `غ ي ر:B001/m01`; repair of their equipment `غ ي ر:B001/m03`; practical aid `ع و ن:B001/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Compassion is the motive, progressive nurture and guardianship are the continuing operations, and food, equipment, and assistance are the practical means. Blessing names the resulting condition of the cared-for dependent.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:2","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Declared Identity and Public Presence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.","parent_surprising_reach":"The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.","key":"A","name":"Naming, Invocation, and Direct Address","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A speaker identifies the addressee by name and turns that name into invocation, oath, or direct appeal.","active_motifs":"name and naming `س م و:B005/m01`; a namesake or comparable bearer of a name `س م و:B005/m02`; the divine name `ء ل ه:B002/m01`; oath and vocative formulas using that name `ء ل ه:B002/m02`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` and `ٱللَّهِ` (`س م و`, `ء ل ه`); 1:2 `لِلَّهِ` (`ء ل ه`)","synthesis":"Naming establishes reference, while oath and vocative use convert reference into an active relation between speaker and addressee. The namesake motif defines identity comparatively by asking who else can bear the same designation.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2"]},{"parent_channel":"Declared Identity and Public Presence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.","parent_surprising_reach":"The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.","key":"C","name":"Reputation and Poetic Exchange","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Public speech circulates praise or blame until it becomes a person's audible reputation and social eminence.","active_motifs":"good reputation or public voice `س م و:B008/m01`; social eminence `س م و:B001/m02`; exchanged praise or satire in verse `ه د ي:B011/m01`; the person of many praised qualities `ح م د:B003/m01`; soliciting praise for favors `ح م د:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` (`ح م د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"Poetic exchange is the transmitting action, praise and satire supply opposite evaluations, and public voice is the resulting social state. Eminence describes the vertical effect of a reputation that others cannot easily match.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Declared Identity and Public Presence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Names, utterances, reputation, and bearing make identity or commitment socially legible.","parent_surprising_reach":"The naming and address field reaches from divine invocation into assent, grammatical qualification, poetic exchange, and the ceremonial presence of a reputed captain.","key":"D","name":"The Reputed Captain's Ceremonial Bearing","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A maritime captain appears as a composed, publicly reputed leader whose station is marked by ceremonial scent.","active_motifs":"captain or chief of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; perfume brazier `ع ب د:B012/m01`; calm and dignified bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; good public reputation `س م و:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"The captain supplies the leading role, calm bearing and visibility make that role perceptible, reputation carries it beyond the immediate crew, and the perfume brazier gives the scene a ceremonial object of status.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:5","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.","key":"B","name":"The Visible Leader at the Front","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A conspicuous leader takes the foremost position and draws a herd or procession behind it.","active_motifs":"the foremost guide `ه د ي:B003/m01`; the leading animal followed by the group `م ل ك:B008/m01`; an elevated visible figure `س م و:B002/m01`; a gathered wild-cattle herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; a herd of wild donkeys `ع و ن:B006/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"Visibility allows the leader to be tracked, the foremost position supplies direction, and the gathered animals supply the following participants. Leadership is thus rendered as a spatial relation before it becomes a social one.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:5","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Orientation, Passage, and Breakdown","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement is organized by a course, a guide, and a state of passage, then tested by deviation, engulfment, or loss of mobility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The straight-road scene expands into landmarks, centerlines, and leading animals, while swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial supply a shape-motion analogy for passage into disappearance.","key":"C","name":"Engulfing and Penetrating Passage","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An entity enters, cuts through, or is swallowed by another body or medium until it becomes hidden.","active_motifs":"swallowing and disappearance through a passage `ص ر ط:B002/m01`; disappearance or burial within another medium `ض ل ل:B002/m01`; a stallion rising over and entering the herd `س م و:B003/m01`; a cutting, penetrating sword `ص ر ط:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:6 and 1:7 `ٱلصِّرَٰطَ` / `صِرَٰطَ` (`ص ر ط`); 1:7 `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ض ل ل`)","synthesis":"Swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial share a directional transition across a boundary. Their common outcome is loss of exterior visibility, whether the receiving medium is a throat, a body, or the earth.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Time, Measure, and Final Reckoning","parent_semantic_invariant":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The day expands from ordinary daylight into zenith, lunar marking, personal pace, prolonged residence, and finally the event in which creation rises for sovereign accounting.","key":"A","name":"Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.","active_motifs":"bounded daylight `ي و م:B001/m01`; the sun standing at midday `ق و م:B017/m01`; the overhead sky `س م و:B004/m01`; a lunar station `ن ع م:B007/m04`; a distinguishing sign `ع ل م:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Daylight supplies the bounded interval, zenith gives it an internal midpoint, and sky signs provide a larger celestial coordinate system. Time is read through the positions of bodies that appear to stand overhead.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer","parent_semantic_invariant":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","parent_surprising_reach":"Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.","key":"C","name":"Reciprocal Gift and Beneficent Transfer","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A gift moves toward a valued recipient as an expression of affection, mercy, or benefit.","active_motifs":"affectionate gift-giving `ه د ي:B004/m01`; conferred benefit `ن ع م:B001/m02`; mercy enacted as benefaction `ر ح م:B001/m02`; transfer into another's ownership `م ل ك:B002/m02`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Affection supplies the social motive, the gift supplies the transferred object, ownership change completes the act, and benefaction names its intended effect on the recipient.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:4","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Property, Measure, and Legitimate Transfer","parent_semantic_invariant":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","parent_surprising_reach":"Financial debt and equal coinage join a livestock market and its stray property, while compensation and substitution offer a regulated alternative to uncompensated loss.","key":"D","name":"Consecrated Offering","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Livestock or goods are selected from owned wealth and directed toward the sanctuary as an act of worship.","active_motifs":"consecrated offering to the sanctuary `ه د ي:B005/m01`; livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`; the worshipped deity `ء ل ه:B001/m02`; worshipful submission `ع ب د:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `ٱللَّهِ` and 1:2 `لِلَّهِ` (`ء ل ه`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","synthesis":"Livestock supplies the transferable material, consecration fixes its destination, and worship supplies the relation that distinguishes an offering from an ordinary gift.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"A","name":"Kinship, Fosterage, and Continuing Care","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A child belongs to a network of blood and acquired kin whose caregiver assumes responsibility for nurture and continuity.","active_motifs":"close kinship `ر ح م:B002/m01`; maintaining kin ties `ر ح م:B002/m02`; stepchild `ر ب ب:B005/m01`; foster caregiver `ر ب ب:B005/m02`; progressive child-rearing `ر ب ب:B002/m02`; guardianship `ق و م:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`)","synthesis":"Blood relation supplies one form of belonging, fosterage supplies another, and guardianship turns both into continuing obligations. Progressive nurture is the operation that converts formal relation into lived care.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:2","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"B","name":"Gestation, Recent Birth, and Postpartum Vulnerability","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"The womb contains developing offspring, birth produces a newly delivered mother and new life, and the postpartum body remains vulnerable to pain or retained afterbirth.","active_motifs":"the womb as organ `ر ح م:B003/m01`; the womb as seedbed and vessel `ر ح م:B003/m02`; recently delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; freshness or newness after emergence `ر ب ب:B009/m02`; postpartum uterine pain `ر ح م:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`)","synthesis":"The womb supplies enclosure and generative function, newness marks successful emergence, and the recently delivered ewe embodies the maternal participant. Postpartum pain preserves the bodily cost that follows the transition.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:2"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"C","name":"Marriage and Bride-Conveyance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A marriage contract establishes a new household relation and the bride is formally conveyed to her spouse, accompanied by affectionate transfer.","active_motifs":"marriage contract `م ل ك:B004/m01`; bride conveyed to her husband `ه د ي:B006/m01`; kinship relation `ر ح م:B002/m01`; affectionate gift `ه د ي:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:4 `مَٰلِكِ` (`م ل ك`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","synthesis":"Contract creates the relation, conveyance enacts the transition between households, kinship names the resulting bond, and the gift supplies a reciprocal social gesture around the transfer.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:4","1:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"D","name":"Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A named outsider or dependent receives protected standing through covenant, certification, and a rule of compensatory redress.","active_motifs":"covenant and protected association `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; protected refugee or bearer of safe conduct `ه د ي:B007/m01`; certification or delegated oath `د ي ن:B007/m01`; indemnity `غ ي ر:B002/m01`; naming and legal identification `س م و:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:4 `ٱلدِّينِ` (`د ي ن`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` (`غ ي ر`)","synthesis":"Naming identifies the protected person, covenant establishes the relation, certification makes it publicly actionable, and indemnity defines the consequence of violating that protection.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:4","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Household Continuity and Protected Belonging","parent_semantic_invariant":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mercy root opens into womb and blood relation, while guidance becomes both bride-conveyance and the protected status of a refugee under covenant.","key":"E","name":"Protective Jealousy and Household Defense","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Attachment to household and kin activates vigilance, bodily firmness, and visible anger against intrusion.","active_motifs":"protective jealousy over family `غ ي ر:B004/m01`; frowning irritability `غ ض ب:B007/m01`; bodily strength and firmness `ع ب د:B007/m01`; kin group or community `ق و م:B001/m01`; close kinship `ر ح م:B002/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ` (`غ ي ر`, `غ ض ب`)","synthesis":"Kinship supplies the protected circle, jealousy detects a threatened boundary, firmness supplies defensive capacity, and the frown externalizes readiness to resist.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Herd Formation, Wildlife, and Dispersal","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized by gathering, classification, reproduction, leadership, pursuit, and scattering.","parent_surprising_reach":"The animal field ranges from owned livestock and coherent herds to ostrich, falcon, hyena, and ownerless strays, with womb imagery joining wildlife to generation.","key":"B","name":"Hunt, Flight, and Scattered Routes","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Hunters set out, the pursued group takes flight, and a once-coherent body disperses into people, animals, or routes going in different directions.","active_motifs":"hunters setting out `س م و:B006/m01`; flight, rout, and dispersal `ن ع م:B008/m01`; scattered groups and diverging roads `ع ب د:B010/m01`; a human company `ق و م:B001/m01`; loss of a thing from its holder `ض ل ل:B003/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ن ع م`, `ض ل ل`)","synthesis":"Departure initiates pursuit, flight breaks collective formation, and diverging roads spatialize the resulting separation. Loss names the outcome from the perspective of the group or holder that can no longer retain what fled.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:5","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Herd Formation, Wildlife, and Dispersal","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized by gathering, classification, reproduction, leadership, pursuit, and scattering.","parent_surprising_reach":"The animal field ranges from owned livestock and coherent herds to ostrich, falcon, hyena, and ownerless strays, with womb imagery joining wildlife to generation.","key":"C","name":"Wildlife Generation and Exposure","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"New animal life emerges from reproductive enclosure into an open field populated by birds, herd animals, and predators.","active_motifs":"womb as generative vessel `ر ح م:B003/m02`; ostrich `ن ع م:B006/m01`; falcon or hawk `ع ل م:B006/m01`; male hyena `ع ل م:B007/m01`; recently delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; exposed ownerless animal `ض ل ل:B005/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` and `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ر ب ب`, `ع ل م`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ن ع م`, `ض ل ل`)","synthesis":"The womb and recently delivered ewe supply generation, the ostrich supplies a large bird of the open field, and falcon and hyena supply predatory animal classes. The stray marks the vulnerability that follows emergence beyond enclosure and custody.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:2","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Habitation, Water, and the Living Landscape","parent_semantic_invariant":"Habitation becomes viable when authority, residence, water, weather, and vegetation converge into a sustaining place.","parent_surprising_reach":"The field moves from a city defined by obedience into a camp secured by stored water, then upward to layered clouds, persistent rain, gentle wind, and enduring plants.","key":"C","name":"Sky, Rain, Wind, and Enduring Growth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"The overhead sky forms layered rainclouds, weather persists, a gentle southern wind moves through, and watered vegetation remains green.","active_motifs":"overhead sky `س م و:B004/m01`; cloud and rain `س م و:B004/m02`; layered raincloud `ر ب ب:B008/m01`; persistent cloud or wind `ر ب ب:B007/m02`; gentle south wind `ن ع م:B009/m01`; enduring green plant `ر ب ب:B012/m01`; old established palm `ع و ن:B004/m01`; irrigation `غ ي ر:B001/m02`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `غَيْرِ` (`ن ع م`, `غ ي ر`)","synthesis":"Layered cloud gathers the water-bearing material, persistence and wind describe weather movement, irrigation delivers water to the ground, and green plant and old palm display growth across both season and duration.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:2","1:5","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Bodily Integrity, Posture, and Injury","parent_semantic_invariant":"A living body is read through stance, proportion, strength, visible markers, pain, and loss of function.","parent_surprising_reach":"Uprightness and firmness describe healthy stature, yet the same standing field also holds an intact-looking blind eye, diseased legs, swollen tissue, and a body halted by weakness.","key":"B","name":"Lesion, Swelling, and Localized Pain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A specific body part bears a visible lesion or swelling and becomes the site of persistent pain.","active_motifs":"cleft in the upper lip `ع ل م:B004/m01`; swelling around the eye `غ ض ب:B006/m01`; pain lodged in an organ `ق و م:B019/m01`; postpartum uterine pain `ر ح م:B004/m01`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ` (`غ ض ب`)","synthesis":"Cleft and swelling provide visible signs, while localized pain provides the felt state. The postpartum uterus extends the same diagnostic scene to an internal reproductive organ.","ayah_refs":["1:1","1:3","1:2","1:6","1:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Bodily Integrity, Posture, and Injury","parent_semantic_invariant":"A living body is read through stance, proportion, strength, visible markers, pain, and loss of function.","parent_surprising_reach":"Uprightness and firmness describe healthy stature, yet the same standing field also holds an intact-looking blind eye, diseased legs, swollen tissue, and a body halted by weakness.","key":"D","name":"Bodily Markers of Maturity and Reproduction","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Bodily marks, sexual maturity, reproductive capacity, and age make a living body classifiable.","active_motifs":"pubic hair `ع و ن:B007/m01`; womb as reproductive organ `ر ح م:B003/m01`; middle age `ع و ن:B002/m01`; mature balanced strength `ع و ن:B005/m01`; a distinguishing bodily mark `ع ل م:B002/m03`","ayah_anchors":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`)","synthesis":"Visible hair and marks supply external indicators, the womb supplies reproductive capacity, and age with mature strength locates the body within a life stage.
